Updated 2026-10-29 after the isolated-worktree workflow correction. Triage base: local main `775a34f5`. Sources: [report](provider-reliability-report.md), [original test/coverage index](provider-reliability-baseline.md), and the linked implementation plans. This is a source/evidence reconciliation, not a fresh release-gate run.

The **12 workgroups are the units of work**. The 153 T/A/B/S rows below are scenarios or evidence, not 153 implementation tasks. Every row has exactly one disposition and one primary workgroup. A needs-work row can contain reviewed prerequisites: finish only its stated remainder. Absence of a hosted architecture is a reason not to build one; an external historical fix is not proof of a current Terno bug.

Each new child uses T3 `delegate_task` with `isolation: "worktree"`, a self-contained brief and unique E2E_PORT/WEB_PORT. The initial nonoverlapping wave was W01 send/copy, W10 MIME-only compatibility and W11 Gmail fixture. Gmail and the first W12 diagnostic privacy slice are now landed. W12 diagnostics/console privacy, W01 durable Sent-copy recovery and W06 live-process uncertain-mutation replay guards are landed. W03 Gmail catch-up and W12 background typing-recovery tests are landed; W06 queued UID-reset safety is landed with13 new regressions against existing protection. The attempted W11 incoming-fixture child failed at model capacity before checks; W03 owns any necessary direct-caller route updates. W10 remains parked unlanded after two reviews; W05 saved-Gmail activation is landed; later W05/core/store slices remain serial with W01/W03/W02. IMAP IDLE and SMTP capability slices run separately on their distinct protocol files. Children do not spawn children or edit main. Unknown applicability is investigated before implementation; it is not a reason to invent a service.

Per change: short spec, meaningful failing test, fix and atomic local commits with the AGENTS footer. Run touched-module/direct-caller test files, touched-package types/lint, covered UI spec/visual light+dark checks, and only the affected perf file for a changed hot path. No app build, packaged smoke, full tests/e2e/perf, quiet-window protocol or broad gate replay per fix. Codex code review runs on the worktree, **at most two rounds per series**. Fix round1 findings with regressions; after round2 fix only high-severity correctness/data-loss findings and backlog everything else below.

The orchestrator lands one branch at a time: rebase onto current local main without fetch/merge commits, resolve conflicts and rerun targeted tests/package types, then `merge --ff-only`. If main moves, rebase/retry without force/reset. Update this worklist in a separate docs commit after landing and remove only the finished worktree/branch. Do not push. After active needs-work groups land, announce one final milestone and run typecheck/lint/test/e2e/web/ui-perf/perf plus dist:mac and packaged smoke once; fix failures in small worktrees.

## Scenario dispositions

“Covered” records the cited finite reviewed change, not a fresh current-main passing test. “Already protected” points to existing regression protection; if the specific boundary lacks a test, the workgroup adds that targeted test before changing behavior. Outlook-only APIs are deferred even when their general invariant is covered elsewhere.

### W01

| ID | Scenario / evidence | Disposition | Evidence or remaining boundary |
|---|---|---|---|
| T01 | Remote success versus local completion (P0 candidate) | covered by an existing reviewed change | Plans26/31: accepted-send completion and uncertain-commit fencing, 6331fbd/a7c784a; independent review clear. |
| T07 | Sent copies and partial recipients (P1 gap) | covered by an existing reviewed change | Plan65 durable Sent-copy and independent bounded recipient receipts are landed through13fa734a. Actual SMTP accepted/rejected/unknown outcomes survive SQL/IDB reopen; explicit rejected-only recovery uses one claimed child and the exact surviving sealed MIME/envelope provenance, independent of appendToSent. Acceptance proof survives detail retirement. Settings saved/unknown Clear is fixed by the authorized follow-up. Original R2 visible/queued snackbar P2 remains explicit backlog; no remote exactly-once or delivery promise. |
| A31 | Partial SMTP acceptance and ambiguous post-DATA failure need a richer send result | covered by an existing reviewed change | Landed Plan65 validates complete/disjoint SMTP reports after the synchronous acceptance latch, otherwise records unknown. SQL/IDB persist bounded occurrence/outcome detail independently of Sent copies; recovery deliberately creates one idempotently claimed operation for proven rejections only, using pinned immutable wire. Real RCPT/DATA, unknown/lost-ACK, completion failure, retirement and reopen controls preserve no-resend fences. |
| A32 | Filing Sent and deleting a sent draft are post-delivery operations | covered by an existing reviewed change | W01 dacb8844/9ac1a7b6 separates SMTP acceptance from durable Sent-copy pending/rejected/unknown/saved state and exact-byte repair/reconciliation; 314 targeted tests and two review rounds. Existing local immutable queue preserves draft consumption. The authorized saved/unknown Settings Clear follow-up is now fixed; independent acceptance proof remains after cleanup. |

Terno has no Send Undo/per-send cancellation control; agent/draftless RPC calls do not acquire a generic exactly-once guarantee. |

## Active high-severity IMAP identity blocker

The A28/S11 real-wire evidence is committed only on the unlanded evidence branch (`89d3bce4`). Root accepted the smallest conservative policy: durable unresolved bindings retain cached content and queued intent, withhold speculative locator adoption, and defer/refuse rather than acknowledge an empty dispatch. Existing root release configuration may be consumed opaquely by the normal build; credential values must never enter diagnostics or chat, and no empty or fabricated client configuration may substitute for the intended build.

Three isolated, disjoint focused series have started:

1. **Storage upgrade contracts:** retained category references, historical IDB schemas and uncertain-upgrade fixtures. Preserve all identities, rows, ownership and atomicity assertions. Any actual migration change requires an executed regression and targeted native/WASM/IDB checks.
